- **phicoding-design**: 框架与抽象的设计方法。从最小实现出发找准主干，把每个底层抽象绑定到最小实现的某个关键要素，再按固化 / 可替换 / 可新增三层拆解——固化写死、可替换走继承与多态、可新增走组合。含设计哲学与设计规范全文、找主干的判据、以及完整的设计顺序。WHEN：要设计新框架、新增抽象、决定分层、判断一块该不该抽、或重构既有结构时加载。 ([wlxms/dsh-phicoding](https://github.com/wlxms/dsh-phicoding/tree/HEAD/dsh-plugins/dsh-phicoding/skills/phicoding-design))
- **phicoding-level**: 信息等级律——依赖图上高等级可取低等级的信息，反之不可。它等价于「依赖单向」，但给出了「谁能看谁」这个可判定判据。含源码引用判据、推与拉的不对称（低可推、不可拉）、以及「底层要依赖上层」的四步决策树与注入面的收窄方法。WHEN：出现底层要依赖上层、注入太深、判断一次访问是否越界、或设计管理器与组件关系时加载。 ([wlxms/dsh-phicoding](https://github.com/wlxms/dsh-phicoding/tree/HEAD/dsh-plugins/dsh-phicoding/skills/phicoding-level))
- **phicoding-shape**: 可复用的结构形态库。核心是一条定理——在「主干只知抽象 + 类型替代枚举 + 信息单向」下，任何开放的集合都必然表现为「按类型索引」——以及五个已验证形态：单例、事件、管线、修正累积、账本加变化。含泛型静态槽的统一清理手法与「状态与变化必须分开且状态只有一份」。WHEN：要写注册表、事件系统、管线、管理器、属性或修正系统时加载。 ([wlxms/dsh-phicoding](https://github.com/wlxms/dsh-phicoding/tree/HEAD/dsh-plugins/dsh-phicoding/skills/phicoding-shape))

- **phinite-tool-builder**: Write Python tool nodes for the Phinite.ai agent workflow platform. Use this whenever the user mentions Phinite, phinite.ai, Phinite Aura, an agent graph, a master or child agent node, session variables, capture variables, or asks for a tool that returns {"output": ..., "capture_variables": ...}. Also use when writing any Python function whose entrypoint is main(inputs, env_variables), when debugging a tool that fails inside Phinite's sandbox, or when adding a tool to an existing agent node. Trigger even if they only say "write me a tool for my agent" without naming the platform. ([virtualvasu/phinite-claude-plugin](https://github.com/virtualvasu/phinite-claude-plugin/tree/HEAD/skills/phinite-tool-builder))
- **phinite-workflow-designer**: Design agent workflows for the Phinite.ai platform — agent graphs, master and child agent nodes, edges, input and capture variables, session state, node prompts and variable descriptions, and debugging a run that misbehaves.
